<span class="ct-node-label">Music</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Music is both an art form and a global system for moving emotion, identity, money and technology. At its simplest, it is organised sound shaped by rhythm, pitch, texture, silence and cultural meaning; in practice, it is also a live experience, a recorded product, a social signal, a memory trigger, a livelihood and a political argument.</span>
</span>
